[iOS] Guard nil items in SettingsTableViewController observer

On Chrome for iOS, applying synced preferences from another device (such
as updating the bottom address bar setting during Synced Set Up) crashes
Chrome when the main Settings controller has not yet loaded its table
model or is running on iPad where the address bar setting row is
omitted.

SettingsTableViewController registers preference observers during
initialization, including for the bottom address bar preference and
memory debugging tools. However, the address bar row is only created on
iPhone after the table model loads, and the memory debugging row is only
created when memory debugging is enabled. When an observed boolean
preference changes while its row item is nil, booleanDidChange attempts
to place nil into an Objective-C array to reconfigure cells, throwing an
uncaught NSInvalidArgumentException.

Skip cell updates in booleanDidChange when the table model has not been
created yet or when the corresponding item was not added to the model,
and add unit tests covering preference updates before model creation and
when optional rows are absent.

Before: http://b/572681651#attachment85917172
After: http://b/572681651#attachment85915772
Screencast: http://b/572681651#attachment85917608

fe36c5787544a774c3e98615813b8df48417a56a [ diff ] 
[Blink] Fold CanvasResource into BaseRenderingContext2D::Resource

BaseRenderingContext2D is the sole remaining consumer of CanvasResource,
which is now a thin wrapper around gpu::ClientImage tracking the acquire
sync token and lost state per resource, and every BaseRenderingContext2D
member and method touching it is private and out-of-line.

Fold CanvasResource into a private nested
BaseRenderingContext2D::Resource class defined in
base_rendering_context_2d.cc (matching
CanvasNon2DResourceProvider::Resource), allowlist gpu::ClientImage in
audit_non_blink_usage.py alongside gpu::SharedImagePool, and delete
platform/graphics/canvas_resource.{h,cc}.

Revert CHECK_EQ to DCHECK_EQ in GestureProvider GetBoundingBox

Revert the CHECK_EQ macro in GestureProvider::GetBoundingBox back to
DCHECK_EQ. The CHECK macro was added in a recent bulk conversion and
triggered crashes. Reverting to DCHECK_EQ avoids crashing while
maintaining debugging assertions in debug builds.

e5250998e20660d43607dc308e297ebc398e93c4 [ diff ] 
[Blink] Add unit tests for BaseRenderingContext2D lost-resource handling

Adds unit tests covering lost-resource behavior across all 2D canvas
SharedImage pool modes:
- Accelerated draw replaces a lost resource even when
  BaseRenderingContext2D holds the only remaining ref.
- Losing one of two concurrent ExportedCanvasResources prevents the
  second, cleanly released export from recycling the shared resource.
- Losing an ExportedCanvasResource prevents a concurrently held
  Snapshot() from recycling the shared resource when dropped later.
- CPU-raster GPU-composited draw replaces a lost resource in
  EndWriteAccess() even when BaseRenderingContext2D holds the only ref.
- Software-compositor exports recycle cleanly released resources and do
  not recycle lost resources.

[webauthn] Implement OS-UV-first flow in LocalChangePinControllerImpl

Implement IsChangePinFlowAvailable(), OS user verification via
DeviceAuthenticator, PIN format toggling, cancellation, and PIN updates
via GpmPinService::SetPin() in LocalChangePinControllerImpl.

When OS user verification is available, StartChangePin() prompts the
user immediately with zero validity period and transitions to
kGPMChangePin on success. Platforms or devices without OS user
verification fail closed until the GAIA reauth fallback lands in the
next change.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK for WebAppSyncBridge database opened status

Revert the CHECK macro back to DCHECK when checking
database_->is_opened() in WebAppSyncBridge::BeginUpdate. When
BeginUpdate is invoked before the database is opened, CHECK evaluated to
false and caused crashes in release builds. Using DCHECK prevents fatal
crashes in release builds while preserving debugging assertion
capability in DCHECK-enabled builds.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/7a9cc1ccaf800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[a11y-image-desc] Fix renderer UKM flags and EmptyBy histograms

`AXImageAnnotator::OnImageAnnotated` sets UKM `OCR` and `Description`
flags before filtering blank strings, and checks for empty results
before inspecting annotation text, leaving `EmptyBy*` histograms unused
and counting blank OCR entries as text hits.

- Set UKM `OCR` and `Description` flags only after confirming non-empty
  text remains after trimming.
- Record `EmptyBy*` versus `SuccessBy*` histograms after filtering blank
  annotations, update renderer `Accessibility.ImageLabels.*` summaries
  and owners (`rhalavati@chromium.org`), and extend expiries to
  2027-11-23.

[Blink] Notify canvas listeners before low-latency frame dispatch

e9ee14a5e344f265c0932c95e121f4442800ffe2

tree
e24dfe4cc1eab9b1c2f2d2129fa1fe21e5663274
parent
7f8db3811a50dd2bda10eb71ff041752086f9e70 [ diff ] 
[Blink] Notify canvas listeners before low-latency frame dispatch

HTMLCanvasElement::DoDeferredPaintInvalidation() returns early for
low-latency (desynchronized) canvases [1], delegating both low-latency
frame dispatch and CanvasDrawListener notification to
HTMLCanvasElement::PostFinalizeFrame() [2].

However, PostFinalizeFrame() dispatched the low-latency frame via
PaintRenderingResultsToResource() before calling
NotifyListenersCanvasChanged() [2], violating the invariant documented
on NotifyListenersCanvasChanged() that listeners must capture the frame
prior to compositing because compositing may discard or clear the
drawing buffer (e.g., WebGL with preserveDrawingBuffer=false).

This stayed latent because existing low-latency
PaintRenderingResultsToResource() paths [3] did not consume or clear the
back buffer, but exporting directly from DrawingBuffer in a follow-up
CL will swap [4] and mark the back buffer for clear [5][6] when
preserveDrawingBuffer=false.

Move the NotifyListenersCanvasChanged() call in PostFinalizeFrame()
before the low-latency dispatch block, matching the non-low-latency
ordering [7], and add a unit test verifying listeners are notified
before the low-latency frame is dispatched.

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/core/html/canvas/html_canvas_element.cc;l=827-834;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/core/html/canvas/html_canvas_element.cc;l=734-755;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[3] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/modules/webgl/webgl_rendering_context_base.cc;l=2250-2276;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[4] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/platform/graphics/gpu/drawing_buffer.cc;l=571-592;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[5] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/platform/graphics/gpu/drawing_buffer.cc;l=647-649;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[6] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/platform/graphics/gpu/drawing_buffer.cc;l=736-738;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f
[7] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:third_party/blink/renderer/core/html/canvas/html_canvas_element.cc;l=305-308;drc=1c6e60e79bebafc52177ba4e702fc4bc8e39ab6f

Revert CHECK_CURRENTLY_ON in ScopedProfileKeepAlive destructor

ScopedProfileKeepAlive objects can be destroyed off the UI thread in
certain asynchronous callback or task destruction flows, such as when
callbacks run on the IO thread. Using CHECK_CURRENTLY_ON in the
destructor triggers crash dumps in release builds when destruction
happens off the UI thread. Revert this check back to a
DCHECK_CURRENTLY_ON.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/c8f975b888400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

cfcc8063ff80b03a710c7d58c121d7f36c3fbed6
parent
1c9bdd160de548791d1211d3db459ef5dddb6e91 [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Keep descendants_and_self local to constructor

AddressHierarchyConstraints::descendants_and_self_ (along with
is_custom_hierarchy_available_for_country) is only needed during
construction to compute pairwise field-type incompatibilities.

Keep both as constructor-only temporaries in
AddressHierarchyConstraints so cached instances do not retain
unnecessary per-country map state in memory. Passing
descendants_and_self by const reference to
InitializeIncompatibilities() also allows inlining the read-only
flat_map::at() lookups without risking operator[] reallocations.

This refactoring is a functional no-op.

636755eca9721fe7a9d67023d02c83bd5cd62f5b

Wed Nov 25 08:21:53 2026
tree
7da0ac8f6556f58eefe5370d292cbffcf77c584b
parent
9e475416d94de03fd0f9b633172738e6bd5446dd [ diff ] 
Strip trailing dot from host when extracting TLD encoding hint

`DetectTextEncoding()` extracts the TLD hint from hint_url.Host() by
taking the substring after `host.rfind('.')`. For a fully-qualified host
with a trailing dot (e.g. "http://example.co.jp./"), `rfind('.')`
matched the trailing dot and produced an empty hint, dropping the TLD
hint and falling back to content-only encoding detection.

Strip a single trailing dot from the host before extracting the TLD so
trailing-dot hosts preserve their TLD encoding hint.

Guarded by a new runtime flag (enabled by default as a kill switch).

[FSA] Revoke testfs mount point in FileSystemAccessLockManagerTest

This CL revokes the global testfs external mount point in
FileSystemAccessLockManagerTest teardown, asserts that external mount
point registration succeeds across File System Access unit test
fixtures, and re-enables
DeserializeHandle_External_SensitiveEntryCheck on Android.

Previously, FileSystemAccessLockManagerTest registered testfs on the
process-wide ExternalMountPoints singleton without revoking it during
teardown. In batched content_unittests runs (such as Android CI),
subsequent tests silently failed to overwrite testfs and cracked
external paths against the stale directory, breaking
DeserializeHandle_External_SensitiveEntryCheck.

checkdeps: Replace deepcopy with DAG Rules, prefix index, and caching

Replace copy.deepcopy(Rules) per directory with zero-copy parent-linked
DAG rule inheritance, O(depth) prefix-map lookup + per-node include
caching, compiled specific_include_rules, and parent-dir caching in
FindFirstAncestorThatRequiresReview.

Also fixes a pre-existing leak where rules.deps_builders kept every
DepsBuilder alive globally to work around deepcopy; Rules now holds a
direct DepsBuilder reference instead.

chrome/browser (64-core Linux, Python 3.13): 60.1s -> 10.7s.

Part 1 of 5 speeding up Python checkdeps.

[browser_actuator] Add FakeBrowserActuatorTaskClient

Adds a fake BrowserActuatorTaskClient in test_support. Consumers of the
task client and their tests can use it to run every task lifecycle
without a server or a transport.

Tests drive the fake with SimulateTaskStarted(), SimulateWorklogUpdate(),
and SimulateTaskCompleted(). These stand for server events and notify
the observer synchronously.

The fake also handles these cases:
- It drops events before StartTask(), worklog updates before
  SimulateTaskStarted(), and events after the task completes.
- It reports OnTaskStarted() at most once.
- It fails the task when no progress arrives within an idle timeout
  (5 minutes by default). SetIdleTimeout() changes it or turns it off.
- SimulateShutdown() detaches like KeyedService::Shutdown(): no more
  observer callbacks. Calling StartTask() after SimulateShutdown() posts
  OnTaskCompleted(kFailed).

It follows the BrowserActuatorTaskClient contract:
- No observer callback runs inside StartTask(), Cancel(), or the
  destructor. Cancel() posts OnTaskCompleted(kCancelled), and is a no-op
  unless a task is running.
- Destroying the fake drops pending callbacks.
- The observer can destroy the fake inside any callback.

The header shows how to return the fake from MockBrowserActuatorService
in tests.

The fake_task_client target is not testonly, so that a follow-up CL can
return the fake from BrowserActuatorService::CreateTaskClient() behind a
development switch. Its visibility is limited to test_support and
//chrome/browser/browser_actuator. Scripted scenarios come in a
follow-up CL.

Bug: b:582753733
Test: components_unittests --gtest_filter=FakeBrowserActuatorTaskClientTest.*

0ba89dba0fe3507bd1c563022f85ad32b898cd5e
parent
ad6286143f71e155038e76ca20958d8748b5f71d [ diff ] 
Add PinUser32AndGdi32InAvailabilityCheck to fieldtrial testing config

Enable the PinUser32AndGdi32InAvailabilityCheck feature in
fieldtrial_testing_config.json on Windows so Chromium test suites
exercise the new codepath. When enabled, child processes are launched
with a switch instructing base::win::IsUser32AndGdi32Available() to load
and pin user32.dll (and its static import gdi32.dll) rather than only
checking win32k lockdown mitigation policy.

Bug: 577860802, 524260376, 575709646

prefetch: Store sent request cookies in PrefetchResponseReader

To support sending the prefetch-time cookie snapshot with the prefetch
activation beacon, retain the final response's request cookies in
PrefetchResponseReader and forward them to NavigationRequest while
clearing them prior to renderer commit. Also enable
include_request_cookies_with_response for SearchPrefetchRequest.

Only run webdriver_bidi_e2e_tests on try for relevant changes

webdriver_bidi_e2e_tests depends on //chrome, so gn analyze selects it
for almost any browser change (e.g. chrome/browser/flag-metadata.json).
Use the new per-test location_filters so that try builders mirroring
Linux Tests (linux-rel) only run it when WebDriver BiDi, ChromeDriver
or the common CDP backend is modified. CI builders and Mega CQ runs
always run it.

Also documents location_filters in docs/infra/cq.md.

Requires the chromium_tests recipe change and an @chromium-luci roll
that adds targets.mixin(location_filters=...).

Related CLs (land in this order):
1. Recipe: https://crrev.com/c/8482456
2. @chromium-luci targets.mixin(location_filters): https://crrev.com/c/8485826
3. chromium/src config for webdriver_bidi_e2e_tests: https://crrev.com/c/8481124
   (needs an @chromium-luci roll that includes CL 2)

tree
dfd72cf9655b3a82dd2fcbcd9aeb9c7009f44442
parent
feab1224b3764a6744bfa9e00d48546580154333 [ diff ] 
[bookmarks] Harden JSON decoding against malformed roots and extreme IDs

Corrupted on-disk bookmark JSON files could previously leave decoded
bookmark trees in an inconsistent ID state in two ways: a permanent
folder dictionary with a wrong type or missing children list returned
success without assigning the permanent node's ID (triggering a startup
crash when loading account bookmarks), and a node ID near the 64-bit
signed integer maximum overflowed subsequent ID allocations so newly
assigned IDs wrapped and collided with existing nodes.

Validate that all three root folder dictionaries have folder type and a
children list before mutating permanent nodes in `BookmarkCodec`, and
bound persisted IDs accepted without reassignment so subsequent ID
reassignments and `max_id` calculations cannot overflow `int64_t`.

60f0acbeb7fb8d5b6c5d4a6489b192f029c7c627 [ diff ] 
[Manta] Log request and response payload sizes and extend expiries

Manta providers currently record status codes and latency without
tracking request or response payload sizes, and their existing
histograms are nearing expiration.

- Centralize `GetMantaMetricTypeName` across Manta metric helpers, log
  serialized request sizes (`RequestSizeBytes`) once per dispatched
  request before authentication, and log decoded HTTP 200 response sizes
  (`ResponseSizeBytes`, `Counts10M`) so local error strings are
  excluded.
- Extend `Ash.MantaService.*` expiries to 2027-11-23 and add
  `rhalavati@chromium.org` as an owner.

tree
c2133494e5c84ae0c3d1ef2c41e27e8a13659b93
parent
363cb33975fa77b7c84877cf90bd8d77f8000475 [ diff ] 
[bookmarks] Add BookmarkNodeIterator and narrow //ui DEPS

Core bookmark utilities and tests still pulled in generic UI
tree-traversal helpers while allowing unrestricted header inclusions
from the UI layer. Providing a bookmark-native depth-first iterator
removes the last UI model dependency inside core bookmarks and lets
include rules restrict UI dependencies to localization, image types,
and clipboard or drag-and-drop helpers.

In this patch, `BookmarkNodeIterator` replaces `ui::TreeNodeIterator`
inside `components/bookmarks`, and `components/bookmarks/DEPS` narrows
`//ui` rules accordingly (isolating clipboard and drag-and-drop headers
to `BookmarkNodeData` and macOS pasteboard files).

Revert CHECK to DCHECK in ImageDownloaderImpl::Download

Evaluating CHECK(account_id.is_valid()) when account_id is invalid
causes crashes in release builds. Revert back to DCHECK to prevent
crashes while preserving debug assertions.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/4d220c2caf800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

Reland "preloading: Update DSE prewarm fieldtrial testing config"

This is a reland of commit 718f40303f51233752672c53d908daa14b8f9775.

Why it was reverted:
Enabling PrewarmPageLoadAndTabSwitchAfterPaintTrigger in
fieldtrial_testing_config.json triggers an MPArch DSE prewarm prerender
in the active WebContents right after page load and first paint,
causing PageLoadMetricsTest#testPageLoadMetricForPrerendering to
observe a prerendering navigation before addPrerender() is called.

What changed in reland:
- PageLoadMetricsTest now disables PreloadingFeatureMap.PREWARM at the
  class level (landed separately in https://crrev.com/c/8845674).
- Disable Prewarm in ClientHintsBrowserTest and UaReductionBrowserTest
  so background DSE prewarm prerenders do not skew global
  ClientHints.FetchLatency_* and Navigation.UserAgentStringType
  histogram counts.

Original change's description:
> preloading: Update DSE prewarm fieldtrial testing config
>
> Replace the expired PrewarmZeroSuggestTrigger testing config entry
> with PrewarmPageLoadAndTabSwitchAfterPaintTrigger across Desktop and
> Android platforms. Removing PrewarmZeroSuggestTrigger is required so
> the new after-paint trigger is not shadowed by
> kPrewarmZeroSuggestTrigger.
>
> Bug: 406378765
> Change-Id: I30c50ae8d1c7d78d668eefc0ea860bb715e2586d
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8819914
> Commit-Queue: Jiacheng Guo <gjc@google.com>
> Reviewed-by: Keita Suzuki <suzukikeita@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1746370}

Include-Ci-Only-Tests: *|chrome_public_test_apk

tree
70b48fe1980ca3efda9857c25e21a313be973190
parent
35699579b5e02edda0c39de02d319abd2edc9c1a [ diff ] 
nearby: Synchronize and drain platform streams before destruction

Fix a heap-use-after-free in nearby::chrome::InputStreamImpl and
nearby::chrome::OutputStreamImpl when BidirectionalStream is destroyed
concurrently with an in-flight Read() or Write() call on another thread.

Previously, ~BidirectionalStream() posted DestroyStreams directly to
task_runner_ without waiting for in-flight Read()/Write() calls to
unwind. DoClose() (invoked via Close() or the stream destructors)
signaled the member base::WaitableEvent to wake a blocked caller, and
DestroyStreams immediately freed InputStreamImpl and OutputStreamImpl
(along with their member WaitableEvents and pending buffers) while the
woken thread could still be executing inside Read() or Write(). In
addition, IsClosed() (checking handle validity) and pending state were
accessed across threads without synchronization.

This change:
- Protects is_closed_, in-flight Read()/Write() counters, and drain
  synchronization with base::Lock in InputStreamImpl and
  OutputStreamImpl, using STACK_ALLOCATED() RAII guards and CHECKs to
  enforce sequence and lifetime invariants.
- Replaces member WaitableEvents with per-call stack-allocated
  base::WaitableEvents and confines all Mojo data-pipe, watcher, and
  pending state mutations to task_runner_, clearing raw pointers before
  signaling callers.
- Adds WaitForInFlightReads() / WaitForInFlightWrites() and closes and
  drains active calls in ~BidirectionalStream() on the destroying thread
  before posting DestroyStreams to task_runner_, avoiding blocking waits
  on task_runner_.
- Adds deterministic unit tests in bidirectional_stream_unittest.cc,
  input_stream_impl_unittest.cc, and output_stream_impl_unittest.cc
  covering destruction during blocked Read()/Write(), direct draining,
  empty writes, concurrent Read()/Write() vs Close() races, and
  concurrent superseding Read()/Write() calls.

Manually verified on a brya Chromebook with an Android phone: URL and
~313 MB file transfers (BT -> WifiLan, both ways), a ~10-min Bluetooth
transfer, BT -> WebRTC upgrade (16.3 MB), and mid-transfer cancellations
with post-cancel recovery all passed with no crashes or CHECK failures.

BYPASS_LARGE_CHANGE_WARNING: Single cohesive stream lifetime fix.
TAG=agy
CONV=21e5320f-19dc-4df4-8670-5537657128c8

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=BidirectionalStreamTest.*:InputStreamImplTest.*:OutputStreamImplTest.*:BluetoothSocketTest.*:WifiLanSocketTest.*

tree
60e8e6d29e468967e637fd9f0bf327353f1e5657
parent
79711ac48267282e3b4f554e20522e8433a7e599 [ diff ] 
WebUI Toolbar: Remove lazy_load.js bundle split

This CL removes the lazy_load.js bundle split introduced in
crrev.com/c/8618512 and folds all toolbar controls back into the
single webui_toolbar.js bundle, keeping webui_toolbar.ts as the
bundle root.

Loading location-bar and other non-navigation controls from an idle
callback caused them to be missing from the first painted toolbar
frames when WebUILocationBar or WebUIToolbar is enabled. Because
almost all controls in lazy_load.js (location-bar, app-menu-button,
avatar-button, glic-button, pinned-toolbar-actions, extensions, and
overflow-button) are needed for the first paint of the full toolbar,
keeping a separate lazy bundle adds complexity without helping the
full WebUIToolbar target.

Revert CHECK_GE to DCHECK_GE in OnVideoStartsInVisibleTab

when the current visible window count is less than the visible tabs

crash reports in release builds while preserving debugging assertion
checks in debug builds.

5cdcd19578e61840c9f13bea7d447bcec74ba43f

tree
e684adcbf6721ecaf66658ca4a6f278829fbeef8
parent
a6755d7fc883ab6791d81cf0600f488a90c41e21 [ diff ] 
[NTP Customization Sync] Persist theme collection image attributions

Before this CL, Chrome saved the current theme collection image to
SharedPreferences without its two attribution lines and its attribution
link. The same was true for the pre-fetched daily refresh image.

An image restored from this record, after a cold start or a daily
refresh rotation, therefore had no attributions. The attributions are
the TalkBack description and tooltip of a theme history entry, and are
synced to other devices.

Now the record is stored as JSON with all fields. It is the JSON a theme
history entry already uses, so the code that reads and writes it moves
from NtpBackgroundDataThemeCollection to CustomBackgroundInfo and is
shared.

The JSON also stores a version, so that a later format change can be
told apart. A record with a version this build doesn't know is ignored.

A record in the old format is still read. It has no attributions.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Clear AXTree extra announcement nodes during root updates
📋 Metadata
commit
34e604149753ec319d84a2e2e5fcd8495908c797
[ log ]
author
Ramin Halavati <rhalavati@chromium.org>
Wed Nov 25 06:39:05 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Nov 25 06:39:05 2026
tree
5c2029c6210d4b4499742091e07177feec40df31
parent
40b3b4d533bf401b48b234c401533c0bb160c24d [ diff ] 
Clear AXTree extra announcement nodes during root updates

On Linux and Windows, AXTree::extra_announcement_nodes_ holds two
generated nodes whose parent pointers point to the tree root. Previously
they were only scheduled for clearing when updating the existing root
and cleared after the tree update finished, so swapping or removing the
root freed the old root first and left dangling parent pointers.

- Detect missing, replaced, destroyed, or updated roots in
  ComputePendingChanges().
- Notify observers and reset extra announcement nodes inside
  AXTree::Unserialize() before the old root can be freed, removing the
  old post-update clearing path.
- Add unit tests covering same-tree root swaps.

AX-Relnotes: n/a

WebGL-on-WebGPU: Enable ANGLE WebGL compatibility mode

Create the ANGLE context with EGL_CONTEXT_WEBGL_COMPATIBILITY_ANGLE so
ANGLE enforces WebGL validation and starts with extensions disabled.
Request GL_OES_EGL_image at startup for swap-buffer imports.

c4249f7cdae1b04fc877ac31e20b193827b49015 [ diff ] 
[HarfRust] Add hb_shim.h for harfrust_capi dispatch

Disable HarfRustShaping in test status and remove the legacy HarfBuzz
"harfrust" shaper-list selection from HarfBuzzShaper, since Blink will
dispatch directly to harfrust_capi instead of going through HarfBuzz's
shaper-list adapter.

Add hb_shim.h with compile-time layout assertions, distinct opaque
handle tags, backend-aware unique_ptr deleters, and inline wrappers for
HarfBuzz and harfrust_capi, keeping IsHarfRustEnabled() false until all
call sites are migrated. Migrate VariableAxesNames::GetVariationAxes()
as the initial consumer and consolidate Blink's BUILD.gn and DEPS rules
for harfbuzz and harfrust_capi.

Enable gn check --check-generated on linux-rel

Set gn_check_generated on ci/Linux Builder, so the compile step of
linux-rel and the other try builders that mirror it runs
"gn check --check-generated" on the compiled targets and fails on
violations. ci/GPU Linux Builder sets the same value, since
linux-rel mirrors both and mirrored specs must match.

The check passes on all compiled targets with this config in led builds
of the affected builders:
- ci/Linux Builder: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664763333775644113
- ci/GPU Linux Builder: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190366168708321
- ci/linux-devtools-frontend-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190364645761585
- ci/linux-separate-renderer-fyi-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190362968461649
- ci/linux-webdriver-bidi-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664185712253247249
- try/linux-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664185705978103265
- try/linux-dcheck-off-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190361094764049
- try/linux_chromium_compile_rel_ng: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190359542474785
- try/linux-no-initial-webui-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190357577267201
- try/gpu-try-linux-nvidia-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190355721539249
- try/linux-separate-renderer-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664190353951461697
- try/linux-webdriver-bidi-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664185710031676161
- try/linux-oi-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664185708016962257
- try/linux-devtools-frontend-rel: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8664185701171754625

dc4985afbd5bfebd688c7c2ca9bec3a21f1f62c8
parent
3521db4892e3ec928c5dbca1636b145bd9be1f9e [ diff ] 
[Android] Return file type icons from downloads.getFileIcon()

On desktop Android chrome.downloads.getFileIcon() always fails with
"Icon not found", so an extension cannot show an icon for a download.
Android has no OS file type icons that IconLoader can use, so it
returned an empty image.

Return the icons that Chrome's own download UI shows instead (image,
audio, video or generic file), so an extension's list of downloads
matches the Downloads page. They are tinted a neutral gray because the
extension's background, light or dark, is not known.

A finished download on Android is a content URI with no file extension,
so its type has to come from its content provider, or from its display
name when the provider only knows a generic type. Those queries can
block, so they stay on the thread pool.

Fail the load for a scale that would give an empty or huge bitmap. Java
cannot create one, and the browser crashes.

Fixed: 579516055
Test: android_browsertests DownloadExtensionTest_FileIcon*
Test: android_browsertests IconLoaderBrowserTest.*
Test: chrome_junit_tests DownloadIconUtilsTest

Catch V8 exceptions in CSS paint and layout worklets

commit
b9d8b3b890ed9e543ba5363e4f35f536d39fc6b1

tree
9a2d9b521f9486bfc0a0952e8fd688e9b6e5db9c
parent
adea1b57a476029c40cfe3af9fa1da3690d970bf [ diff ] 
Catch V8 exceptions in CSS paint and layout worklets

CSSPaintDefinition::Paint calls MaybeCreatePaintInstance before it
starts a v8::TryCatch scope. If the constructor of a paint class
throws an exception during synchronous paint, the exception stays on
the V8 isolate. When the worklet scope ends, the exception goes to page
script and can cause a crash.

CSSLayoutDefinition has the same problem. CreateInstance,
Instance::Layout, and Instance::IntrinsicSizes call worklet
constructors and callbacks without a v8::TryCatch scope. If one of
these calls throws an exception during synchronous layout, the
exception stays on the V8 isolate and can cause a crash.

This change prevents the crash:

- In CSSPaintDefinition::Paint, check script_state_->ContextIsValid()
  before ScriptState::Scope. Move v8::TryCatch before
  MaybeCreatePaintInstance so that it catches exceptions from both the
  constructor and paint().
- In CSSLayoutDefinition, add v8::TryCatch with SetVerbose(true) to
  CreateInstance, Instance::Layout, and Instance::IntrinsicSizes. Put
  v8::TryCatch outside V8RunMicrotasksScope so that microtasks do not
  run when a callback throws an exception.
- Add web platform tests and a PaintWorkletTest unit test to check that
  these exceptions do not go to page script.

Revert "[Android] Honor actionless snackbar durations"

This reverts commit 00787f8d0aacaf75a0a7f6108fd92efb7cc475e5.

Reason for revert: Broke compile on android-desktop-x64-compile-rel (ExclusiveAccessBubbleTest.java calls ExclusiveAccessBubble.update(String) instead of update(String, boolean)).

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-desktop-x64-compile-rel/101480/overview

Original change's description:
> [Android] Honor actionless snackbar durations
>
> Actionless TYPE_ACTION snackbars used the ten-second action minimum even
> when callers requested shorter durations. Apply that minimum only when
> an action button is present, using action text so null action data
> remains valid. Preserve notification timing and accessibility
> extensions.
>
> This lets fullscreen guidance use its requested 3800 ms while retaining
> TYPE_ACTION and high priority. Keep duration policy coverage in
> SnackbarTest and use direct assertions for fullscreen creation/update
> properties and competing snackbar ordering.
>
> Bug: 553126935
> Test: ExclusiveAccessBubbleTest, SnackbarCollectionUnitTest
> Test: SnackbarTest on Android API 34
> Test: git cl upload (presubmit)

Rename {ShouldRouteTo->Is}FirstPartyVietnameseEngine().

Unlike other ShouldRouteTo...() in the vicinity, this is a pure
engine-ID check, without consultation of use_ime_service_
(tests-only, with vs without IME Service) or virtual keyboard
state (affecting in-prod routing logic). This rename aligns it
with other Is...() engine-ID checks.

BUG=589462840

f7231c3061f6d0dca628ff479eff904e332498d4 [ diff ] 
[Android] Honor actionless snackbar durations

Actionless TYPE_ACTION snackbars used the ten-second action minimum even
when callers requested shorter durations. Apply that minimum only when
an action button is present, using action text so null action data
remains valid. Preserve notification timing and accessibility
extensions.

This lets fullscreen guidance use its requested 3800 ms while retaining
TYPE_ACTION and high priority. Keep duration policy coverage in
SnackbarTest and use direct assertions for fullscreen creation/update
properties and competing snackbar ordering.

Bug: 553126935
Test: ExclusiveAccessBubbleTest, SnackbarCollectionUnitTest
Test: SnackbarTest on Android API 34
Test: git cl upload (presubmit)

[Adaptive UI][CredMan] Migrate NoPasskeysBottomSheet to AdaptiveUiEntry

Passes AdaptiveUiController.from(window) from
NoPasskeysBottomSheetBridge to NoPasskeysBottomSheetCoordinator and
NoPasskeysBottomSheetMediator. When the controller is attached
(AndroidAdaptiveUiTransitions enabled), NoPasskeysBottomSheetMediator
shows a TRANSIENT AdaptiveUiEntry with HEADER_ICON, TITLE, bold-origin
DESCRIPTION, OK / Use another device buttons, sheet accessibility
strings, and UserCriticalFeature.TOUCH_TO_FILL_NO_PASSKEYS across
BOTTOM_SHEET and MODAL_DIALOG containers. When the controller is null,
it keeps the existing NoPasskeysBottomSheetContent path.

Screenshots:
- Flag OFF (legacy sheet): https://screenshot.googleplex.com/T5mT9PSzBTmwCQD
- Flag ON (AdaptiveUiEntry sheet): https://screenshot.googleplex.com/Z6rqBaCg4MjdhFd
- Dialog on AL: https://screenshot.googleplex.com/xy3CnzV2TNUVGxA

0af6b1f6c1cb8adf70f5af6ad02c7ec7ac3087ef
parent
8d5241302804647c493e4ffd8dfc10d5b893d1a8 [ diff ] 
base: Use size_bytes() in VectorBuffer::RangesOverlap

VectorBuffer::RangesOverlap() added the element count (span::size())
instead of the byte length (span::size_bytes()) to uintptr_t addresses,
under-computing range end addresses whenever sizeof(T) > 1 and causing
CHECK(!RangesOverlap(from, to)) in MoveConstructRange() to miss
overlapping spans. The bug was introduced in
https://crrev.com/c/5655271.

Also make RangesOverlap() treat empty spans as never overlapping (even
when pointing into the middle of the other span), and have
MoveConstructRange() check that the sizes match before checking for
overlap, so that a size mismatch is reported as such.

Evaluate `FontFace` range descriptors once and validate bounds
📋 Metadata
commit
eb9f319b36af865bf5e909ad027f9ca0aa46e1d4
[ log ]
author

Wed Nov 25 04:50:19 2026
tree
f6fae6f2a610893be693f121e453559aafe51af0
parent
8cd66b8c25e646e30c08631726d5828c5234e8c5 [ diff ] 
Evaluate `FontFace` range descriptors once and validate bounds

`FontFace::GetFontSelectionCapabilities()` previously called
`ComputeValueInCanonicalUnit(EnsureLengthResolver())` multiple times per
endpoint for stretch, oblique, and weight ranges. Evaluating a unit such
as `1ch` can trigger a pending web font load and invalidate fallback
lists, causing subsequent evaluations in the same call to return
different values (e.g., 0). Additionally, weight ranges only checked
`weight_from < 1` and `weight_to > 1000`, and did not guard against NaN.

When all fields evaluated to 0, the resulting
`FontSelectionCapabilities` collided with the `HeapHashMap` empty key in
`FontFaceCache`, causing Oilpan to skip tracing the
`CSSSegmentedFontFace` and leading to a UAF.

Evaluate each endpoint once, validate that both weight range endpoints
are in [1, 1000] (also rejecting NaN) and stretch/oblique values are not
NaN, and `CHECK` in `FontFaceCache::CapabilitiesSet::AddFontFace` that
the capabilities key is not the empty HashTable value.

Guarded by a new runtime flag (enabled by default as a kill switch).

tree
fe44f48c8d15c017d7277f6e2591958713bddcf3
parent
dea4deb7f67db1b466ba5b18a88c2b02cba530d7 [ diff ] 
[views] Don't embed LTR-first mixed-direction window titles as RTL

On non-Windows platforms, AdjustStringForLocaleDirection() wraps any
string that contains a strong RTL character in RLE/PDF when the UI is
LTR. Widget::UpdateWindowTitle() applied it to the whole window title,
so a page title like "Of contraseñas, <Hebrew>, and 密码" became an RTL
embedding, and window managers showed it as
"and 密码 - Chromium ,<Hebrew> ,Of contraseñas".

As a narrow stopgap for LTR UIs while full FSI...PDI composition
isolation is tracked separately, skip the adjustment when the title's
first UAX #9 P2 strong {L, AL, R} character is already L. Ignoring
LRE/RLE/LRO/RLO ensures LRE-wrapped RTL URLs from
NavigationEntryImpl::GetTitleForDisplay() still receive an outer LRM.
Titles whose first strong character is RTL, and RTL UIs, keep the
existing behavior.

parent
a3f395c85892635f253502982bade81c4b09a1a2 [ diff ] 
[iOS][Actor] Let ActorTask ask the user for clarification

A `ClarificationInterrupt` puts the task in `kWaitingOnUser`, as on
Desktop, and asks its question through the intervention delegate. The
user's typed answer is replied as `kAnswered` in an `InterruptReply`,
which replaces `InterruptResponse` as the task's reply so that it can
carry text. Pause, stop, delegate errors, and held interrupts behave as
for confirmations.

Current intervention delegates reply `kUnsupported` until the
clarification UI lands. `GeminiActuationHandler` does not send
clarifications yet.

cannot depend on chrome/browser/ttc. Move PointerContextCapturer, which
captures the screenshot and page content the tool's answer is built

change.

tree
41ec5c80b205cd2a8c2fa283a4707f54313e1949
parent
f8f3959550f67444452415c3dc225cb47ca5698b [ diff ] 
[Clipboard] Restrict ClipboardHostImpl::ReadFiles to kCopyPaste buffer

ClipboardHostImpl::ReadFiles() previously checked only IsPasteAllowed()
before reading file paths from the caller-supplied ClipboardBuffer and
granting the renderer process read access via
PrepareDataTransferFilenamesForChildProcess() and
FileInfosToDataTransferFiles().

On platforms that support ui::ClipboardBuffer::kSelection (such as Linux
X11/Ozone), a renderer with transient user activation could invoke
ClipboardHost::ReadFiles(kSelection) to read filenames from the selection
buffer and obtain ChildProcessSecurityPolicy::GrantReadFile() permissions
even though selection paste is plain-text only.

Reject any buffer other than ui::ClipboardBuffer::kCopyPaste in
ClipboardHostImpl::ReadFiles() and return an empty ClipboardFiles struct
immediately, matching the blink::mojom::ClipboardHost::ReadFiles contract
while preserving middle-click selection text paste and standard
copy/paste file reads.

Bug: 553272482

[glic] Add glicPrivate.hasActiveActuationTab and actor tab activation

Previously, ActivateTabWithConversation relied solely on passive
GlicInstance registration and bound tabs, failing when an actuation task
started before opening a Glic instance. Meanwhile, callers checking
whether an active local actuation tab exists had to rely on
glicPrivate.hasConversation(), which reports passive side panel presence
and produces both false negatives (when Glic is closed) and false
positives (when the conversation is open passively without an active
actuation task).

- Add NormalizeConversationId, GetNormalizedGeminiConversationId, and
  FindActiveGeminiActuationTab in glic_actuation_locality_tracker.{h,cc}
  (shared with the follow-up locality tracker CL) to resolve the live
  controlled tab of the most recently started Gemini ActorTask matching
  a conversation ID (normalizing whitespace, ASCII case, and an optional
  leading "c_" prefix).
- Add chrome.glicPrivate.hasActiveActuationTab(conversationId) backed by
  GlicInstanceCoordinator::HasActiveActuationTab, leaving
  hasConversation() / IsConversationPresent() unchanged for passive
  GlicInstance presence.
- Update ActivateTabWithConversation to focus an active ActorTask's tab
  before falling back to GlicInstance lookup.

Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=*GlicActuationLocalityTracker*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*GlicMessagingFullyEnabled*
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter=*GlicPrivateApiFullyEnabled*

68b0d75c2267c49e70bf228448669d2dfcb9ea0a [ diff ] 
WebUI NTP: Enable build-time bundled V8 code caching for New Tab Page

This CL enables build-time bundled V8 code caching for
chrome://new-tab-page/ behind kWebUIBundledCodeCacheForNewTabPage. It
generates code caches for both the lazy and eager composebox bundles and
registers the one matching the served bundle.

Precompiled bytecode lets startup and new-tab loads skip script parsing.
Shipping both caches (5.5 MB in resources.pak on Linux) keeps the flag
orthogonal while kNtpLazyLoadComposebox is under experiment. NTP
renderers disallow V8 flag overrides to accept the cache and bypass the
warm spare renderer; the numbers below include that cost:
- Local Linux browser-startup (--initial-webui, N=96): NonEmptyPaint3
  -14.0 ms (-3.2%) with the flag on vs. off.
- Pinpoint linux-r350-perf: NTP Load Startup -32.0 ms (-13.9%) Cold /
  -36.0 ms (-15.7%) Warm.
- Pinpoint mac-m1-pro-perf: NTP Load Startup -19.0 ms (-9.3%) Cold /
  -17.5 ms (-8.7%) Warm.

d49cdb8add6b9b92e220aed9831972a7fc1e5ca3

tree
96461de229dadafbecd85714b79b63fe48656435
parent
d71bb73e32570c515ab4ef1af6995597a179ffc8 [ diff ] 
Keep display capture callbacks tied to their originating requests

Cancelling a display capture request can leave its picker result, DLP
check, or device lookup in flight. A late callback can then apply the
old selection or decision to the replacement request at the queue front.
Renderer-supplied request IDs can be reused, so they cannot distinguish
these requests reliably.

Carry a weak pointer to the pending request from picker creation through
DLP and device lookup, and discard results for requests that are no
longer at the queue front. Start the next request when needed without
restarting a replacement that is already being processed. This also
covers stale picker and device-lookup callbacks outside ChromeOS.

Add coverage for reused IDs, stale picker success and errors, stale DLP
allow and deny decisions, and cancellation leaving an empty queue.

tree
0b22b37bf9ea5d4eb3d3d43ecf11f9d8000bf08f
parent
541191afe31cdc77d52031b24b0c204d8fc179d9 [ diff ] 
[Reading mode PDFs] Avoid revisiting text runs after links and images

Before this CL, `BuildPageTree()` stepped `text_run_index` back by one
after adding an image, a zero-length link, or a link nested inside an
earlier link. Revisiting the run could create a list item twice, skip
trimming the list marker from the item text, and make list item
character counts underflow.

With this CL, when `kPdfAccessibilityHeuristicEnhancements` is enabled,
`BuildPageTree()` adds every link and image that starts at or before
the current run, so links and images no longer move `text_run_index`
backward. A link nested inside another link now stays in the same
paragraph, which updates the overlapping-links heuristics expected
output.

A follow-up CL does the same for highlights.

[TabStrip] Re-enable MoveFocusedGroupToExistingWindowDoesNotFocus test

BaseTabStripFocusModeTest.MoveFocusedGroupToExistingWindowDoesNotFocus
was disabled in https://crrev.com/c/8761019 due to a CHECK failure in
tab_strip_layout_utils.cc when moving the only unpinned tab group to
another window.

The underlying layout bug in
UnpinnedTabContainerViewHorizontalLayout::CalculateProposedLayout() when
no unpinned children remain visible was fixed in
https://crrev.com/c/8770483, which re-enabled
MoveFocusedGroupToNewWindowDoesNotFocus but left
MoveFocusedGroupToExistingWindowDoesNotFocus disabled. Re-enable
MoveFocusedGroupToExistingWindowDoesNotFocus now that the layout fix has
landed.

Flaky test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium%3Aci/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3ABaseTabStripFocusModeTest%23MoveFocusedGroupToExistingWindowDoesNotFocus%2FAll.Horizontal?q=VHASH%3A458d6b7b4b69b27a

[Bedrock] Migrate c/t:test_support (11/N) consumers in c/b/glic

Add direct deps on previously-transitive targets and remove the
c/t:test_support dep from the test targets in c/b/glic (actor,
browser_ui, e2e_test, media, navigation_capture, selection, suggestions,
test_support, and the top-level browser_tests). Each now depends
directly on the specific granular targets it includes, under the same
platform guards as the sources that require them.

Several glic test targets include glic/public/provider_features.h, which
has restricted visibility and was previously reached transitively via
c/t:test_support. Grant those targets direct visibility and add a direct
dep on c/b/glic/public:provider_features.

Part of migrating //chrome/test:test_support's consumers to direct deps,
in preparation for deleting the test_support group.

Analysis/Plan: https://notes.nickdiego.dev/chromium/bedrocking-chrome-test

Revert CHECK in BrowserView::FocusAppMenu back to DCHECK

Revert the CHECK macro in BrowserView::FocusAppMenu back to a DCHECK and
add a TODO comment. This prevents release build crashes when the
assertion fails while allowing further investigation into why immersive
mode is enabled when app menu focus is requested.
